So:
- `result.suggestions` (Suggestion[]) at run.ts:246 — built from advised outcomes that have a suggestion presumably → push only status==='suggested' → 4.
- `summaryOf` → `suggestions: result.suggestions.length` → 4. ✓ matches api-matrix.
- SessionView.suggestions = lastPayload(events,'suggestions','suggestions') = advised.outcomes = 5 (includes the not-needed one). 

So my e2e assertion used the wrong number for the detail view; it's not a bug, just two different things: summary counts actual suggestions (4), detail view returns all outcomes (5). The UI uses the events/outcomes. Fine — this is a naming ambiguity worth noting but not a defect. Hmm, is it? `SessionView.suggestions: SuggestOutcome[]` vs `RunSummary.suggestions: number` — different semantics for the same name across two endpoints. Mildly confusing but documented types differ. Not a bug. I could note in findings.

Adjust e2e assertion: check verdicts 5 and outcomes 5, with exactly 4 having status 'suggested'.

```
const deletingRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

  if (deletingRef.current.has(id)) return;
  deletingRef.current.add(id);
  try { await deleteSession(id); if (sandbox.sessionId === id) dispatch({type:'reset'}); await refreshSessions(); }
  catch (e) { setHint(...) }
  finally { deletingRef.current.delete(id); }
}, ...)
```

```
before/after delete. Then the row disappears immediately, so a second click can't hit it. That's the actual fix for the race.

```
let edgeProc = null;
let serverProc = null;
let cleaned = false;
function cleanup() {
  if (cleaned) return;
  cleaned = true;
  try { if (edgeProc !== null) edgeProc.kill(); } catch (e) {}
  try { if (serverProc !== null) serverProc.kill(); } catch (e) {}
}
process.on('exit', cleanup);
process.on('SIGINT', () => { cleanup(); process.exit(130); });
```

```text
386| 排查手法很便宜：对每个 `process.env` 键做一次全仓库 grep，命中数为 1（只有模板自己）就是死配置。
387| ## F26. 自己写的渲染器，bug 也得自己抓：嵌套列表被静默压平（2026-10-26）
388| 
389| 技术文档的 Markdown→HTML 渲染器是自己写的（理由：pandoc 与 Python markdown 在本机都不存在，
390| 而文档要在评委机器上用一条命令重建）。测试写完立刻红了一条：
391| 
392| ```
393| expected '<ul><li>一</li><ul><li>一之一</li></ul><li>二</li></ul>'
394| received '<ul><li>一</li><li>一之一</li><li>二</li></ul>'
395| ```
396| 
397| 根因在列表渲染的栈：栈里存的是**标签名**（'ul'/'ol'），而判断写的是 `item.indent < stack[栈顶]`，
398| `3 < 'ul'` 永远为 false，于是「缩进更深就开新列表」这条分支从不执行，嵌套被压平成同一层。
399| 修法是栈里同时存 `{ tag, indent }`。
400| 
401| **教训一**：自己写的东西没有上游社区兜底，测试是唯一的兜底——所以「零依赖」的代价必须用测试密度来付。
402| **教训二**：这个 bug 在排版上**看不出来**，因为文档源里当时没有嵌套列表。
403| 「渲染结果肉眼正常」不等于「渲染器对」，只有构造出那条分支的用例才算数。
404| 
405| ## F27. 同一个事实写在两份文档里，就会长出两个不同的数字（2026-10-26）
406| 
407| 技术文档 4.1.1 写「复现合计 34.5 s」，根 README 的耗时表写「约 40 秒」。两次都是实测、都真实，

411| 
412| **处置规则**：跨文档复用的实测数字，必须在源文档里标注「由哪条命令生成」，并且**改一次就全改**。
413| 更彻底的做法是让文档从命令输出生成（本项目已有先例：Prompt 链快照、界面截图），
414| 但耗时表依赖机器，做不到逐字生成，所以退而求其次——写清楚区间与来源。

Functionality list (from actual product):
- 输入：简历正文（≥30 字）、目标岗位 JD、岗位名称；一键示例；清空；历史会话（列出/载入/删除）。
- 面板：五个面试官人设（技术/HR/业务主管/压力/领域专家），点选增减；播放节奏 即时/慢放。
- 引擎（服务端五阶段）：抽取主张（带原文锚点，锚不到就丢弃并展示）→ 多面试官并发追问（预算+锚定+去重三道闸门）→ 求职者作答（只依据简历，编造引文丢弃）→ 判定（五种，代码级收敛：全答不上→无法回答；需证据但拿不出→证据缺失）→ 改写建议（四道闸门，不能新增简历里没有的数字）。
- 过程可见：SSE 直播逐字输出（29 路 channel）、阶段时间线、遥测（调用/回放/token/事件/追问数）、意图契约摊开（六行+指纹对账+实际 System Prompt）、事件表可续传、断线重连。
- 输出：Markdown 报告下载（五节）、SQLite 落库、卡带回放（29 盘，零 Key 跑通）、一键复现（npm run preview）。
- 工程：四道门禁（typecheck/test/smoke/build）+ 可选（shots/e2e:ui/repro/seed:demo/docs:pdf）；Prompt 链自动导出。

What's NOT there (be honest): 没有登录/多用户、没有 PDF/Word 简历导入（只能粘贴文本）、没有英文简历、没有多场对比、报告只有 Markdown（没有 PDF/Word 导出）、没有成本估算显示、没有移动端适配、live 模式没有把 reasoning 思考内容展示（DeepSeek deepseek-chat 不返回 reasoning_content；用的不是 reasoner 模型）。

That last is a good honest point: 思考可视化 currently only shows text stream, not reasoning (because deepseek-chat doesn't emit reasoning). Optimization: support deepseek-reasoner so the 实时输出 shows actual 思考.

Priorities for optimization:
P1（值得在提交前做，1–2 天）:
1. verdict 对时间线/自我评价类主张的口径偏严 → 提示词补一条口径（需实测对比，可能显著改善判定质量）。

4. PDF/Word 简历导入（pdf 解析需要依赖或调模型——可用「上传后本地提取文本」，但零原生依赖限制…pdfjs 是纯 JS，可作为可选路径；成本中等）。
P2（可选）: 成本估算、多场对比、英文简历、简历原文与建议的一键复制、a11y/键盘操作、移动端布局。
不做: 登录/云同步（涉隐私与合规）、多人协作。

Also mention one known product-口径 issue: 真实模型抽 8–10 条主张、判定几乎全是 unanswerable，建议条数只有 4——可以考虑给判定加「弱证据但可接受」档，或在提示词中要求先判断主张是否属于「可验证事实」还是「主观描述」。

Timeline: 23 天到 11-18。还剩 P5：视频、发布、README 地址、LICENSE。

## 1. 这个项目实现了什么，具体有哪些功能

一句话：**把简历喂给一屋子 AI 面试官，让它们专挑你答不上来的地方追问，然后把结论反推回简历原文。**

一条完整链路的五个阶段（全在服务端，跑起来是并发的）：

| 阶段 | 做什么 | 硬机制 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 抽取主张 | 从简历里抽出一条条「可被追问的主张」 | 每条必须带**逐字原文引文**，锚不到就丢弃并列出来给你看 |
| 多面试官并发追问 | 技术/HR/业务主管/压力/领域专家同时出题 | 三道闸门：追问预算记账、锚定校验、跨面试官去重 |
| 求职者作答 | 一个受限 Agent 只依据简历回答 | 编造的引文会被丢掉，不伪造原文 |
| 判定 | 五种结论：站得住 / 表述过度 / 证据缺失 / 与原文矛盾 / 无法回答 | 两条**代码级收敛**：全答不上→无法回答；要证据却拿不出→降级证据缺失 |
| 改写建议 | 反推回简历，给出改后句子 | 四道闸门，其中一条禁止新增简历里没有的数字 |

界面上你实际能点的东西（我按面板顺序列）：

- **左栏**：简历正文框（<30 字按钮就灰着）、目标岗位 JD、岗位名称、五个面试官 chip（可增可减）、播放节奏（即时/慢放）、`一键示例`、`开始质询`、`清空`、历史会话（列出 / 点开载回 / 删掉）。
- **中栏**：阶段时间线、**实时输出**（模型正在一个字一个字写的那句人话）、意图契约（能摊开六行、能看到「与运行时一致 · 指纹 xxxx」的对账、能展开这位面试官**实际拿到的 System Prompt 原文**）、主张卡片（每条主张 → 原话引文 → 每轮追问 → 作答 → 判定 + 依据 → 改写建议）、被拦下的追问与主张。
- **右栏**：简历原文，点任意引文/提问/建议就在原文里高亮聚焦（显示「第 81–108 字」）。
- **头部**：模式、遥测（调用 N 次 · 回放 N · token N）、事件数、追问数、`下载报告`（Markdown，五个章节）。

工程侧的能力也是产品的一部分：**零 API Key 可离线跑完**（29 盘卡带按提示词指纹寻址）、SQLite 本地落库、SSE 直播可断线续传、`npm run preview` 一个端口起完整产品、Prompt 链自动导出、四道门禁 + 可选门禁（截图/点测/复现预演/技术文档 PDF）。

**现在还没有的**（说清楚，免得你以为有）：登录与多用户、简历 PDF/Word 导入（只能粘贴文本）、英文简历、多场对比、报告导出 PDF/Word、成本估算、移动端适配。live 模式下 `deepseek-chat` 不吐思考内容，所以「实时输出」目前是**正文逐步吐字**，不是思维链。

## 2. 测试是不是打开浏览器把每一样功能都点过的？

**之前不是，现在补上了。** 在这之前有三层，但都盖不住一类问题：

| 层 | 覆盖 | 盖不住的 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 单元/集成（31 文件 / 310 断言） | 领域逻辑、闸门、卡带、事件折叠、HTTP 路由 | 界面没接上 |
| `npm run smoke`（真进程 + 真 HTTP + SSE 收尾） | 接口在真实网络栈下能跑 | 不碰界面 |
| `npm run shots`（真浏览器截图 10 张 + 控制台门禁） | 画面长什么样 | 只出图，不断言行为 |
| **新增 `npm run e2e:ui`** | **真浏览器把 11 个场景、31 项行为点一遍** | 见下方缺口 |

新工具 [tools/ui-e2e/README.md](tools/ui-e2e/README.md) 自起服务（回放模式，不需要 Key）+ 无头 Edge，覆盖：空状态与灰按钮 → 浏览器里手打 87 字简历 → 回放缺卡带时的**响亮报错**（含补救提示）→ 一键示例 → 加减面试官的契约联动 → 跑完一场（卡片/判定/建议条数与服务端逐一对齐）→ 下载报告（真下到 4836 字符）→ 遥测与契约对账、展开 System Prompt → 清空 → 历史列出/载回/删除/清空 → **整轮控制台 0 报错 0 警告（含所有 4xx 响应）**。此外真联网 live 模式也做过真浏览器验收 13/13。

**它当场抓到的两个真缺陷**（都属于「接口是对的、界面没接上」）：

1. **面试官面板顺序跟着点击历史走** —— 先取消 HR 再勾回来，面板变成 `[tech, hiring_manager, hr]`，而追问编号、契约、提示词都按面板顺序生成，**回放模式整盘卡带全部对不上**（现象是「找不到记录」）。已改成集合语义 + `PANEL_ORDER` 归一化。
2. **连点两次删除** —— 第二下打在一个已经不存在的会话上，服务端如实回 404，界面上留一句「删不掉」。已改成先摘列表再发请求。

**仍然没覆盖的**：只有 Edge 一个内核（没测 Chrome/Firefox/Safari）、没有移动端视口、没有多用户并发、真实简历的长尾没系统跑过、live 模式的每个错误分支只验证过一场。

## 3. 还需要优化吗？

需要，但**不建议再铺功能**——离省赛截止 23 天，真正影响得分的是「过程证据」和「判定质量」。我按性价比排：

**P1（值得做，合计约 2–3 天）**
1. **判定口径**：真跑时 DeepSeek 抽 8–10 条主张、几乎全判「无法回答」，连时间线这种有原文直接支撑的也判了 unanswerable（它自己的理由里还写着「站得住」）。这是提示词口径问题，改一条口径就能显著改善观感——而且是很好的「AI 协同修 Bug」素材。
2. **思考可视化**：接 `deepseek-reasoner`，把 reasoning 分片单独显示。管道早就有了（`reasoning` 通道、流式分片、界面标签），只差换模型 + 一个「思考」块。赛题 UX 20% 明确点名「流式、思考可视化」，这条现在只做了一半。
3. **报告导出 PDF/Word**：复用已有 `md2html` + 浏览器打印链路，成本很低，产品感提升明显。

**P2（有余力再说）**：简历 PDF/Word 导入、成本估算、多场对比、英文简历、建议一键复制、键盘可达性。

**明确不做**：登录、云同步、多人协作（碰隐私承诺且不加分）、移动端适配。

## 4. 演示视频：我来录，但请你先授权

我勘过环境了：**EV录屏已装**（找到 `EVCapture`）、**Windows 自动化可用**（能启动并点击真实窗口）、**Windows 内置中文语音可用**（Huihui / Yaoyao 等），但**没有 ffmpeg**。

我的决定是**方案 A（推荐）**：

1. 我先做「录像友好」的准备：一条命令把界面预置成演示状态（示例简历、慢放节奏、大字号），并按 [docs/video/script.md](docs/video/script.md) 的七段分镜准备好每步的**精确操作与停顿**；用系统 TTS 生成中文旁白 `docs/video/narration.wav`（你若不满意自己的声音，后期换掉即可）。
2. 然后**我用 Windows 自动化启动 EV录屏 → 点开始录制 → 驱动浏览器按脚本走完整场 → 停录**，AI 协同那 2 分 20 秒用 `docs/prompt-chain/` 的真实快照与 DSH 对话现场展示（不是事后补写的动画）。
3. 你只需要：**保存好手头工作、离开电脑约 40 分钟**（录屏期间我会接管鼠标键盘）。录像只用内置示例简历、不出现 API Key、不出现你的聊天窗口。

备选 **B（你亲自录）**：我把旁白 + 提词器 + 逐步骤操作清单准备到位，你按提示点，约 15 分钟出片。
备选 **C（完全不占你屏幕）**：用浏览器 CDP 抓帧 + 我自写的 MJPEG-AVI 封装（无需 ffmpeg）合成，但 DSH 那段不是浏览器页面，只能把 Prompt 链渲染成页面上镜，真实感弱一档。

P1-2 (reasoner/thinking) — need to check: does the DeepSeek API model `deepseek-reasoner` work with our live client? Our client sends `response_format: {type:'json_object'}` when json:true. **DeepSeek reasoner does NOT support response_format json_object** (R1 doesn't support JSON output / function calling historically; deepseek-reasoner supports json_object? DeepSeek docs: deepseek-reasoner does not support response_format, temperature, etc.). Also our prompts require JSON output. So switching all stages to reasoner is risky.

Better design: use reasoner only where thinking helps and JSON is tolerable? If reasoner can't do json_object, we can still ask for JSON in the prompt and parse with stripCodeFence (our parseLlmOutput already strips code fences). We could set json:false for reasoner and rely on fence-stripping. But changing `json` flag changes the cassette fingerprint → re-record (fine, seed:demo regenerates).

Also note: the *thinking* content is what the user's UX wants visualized. deepseek-reasoner returns `reasoning_content` in streaming. Our live.ts already handles reasoning_content → yields {type:'reasoning'}. And the web already has kind 'reasoning' in StreamChunk and `channelLabel` shows '思考'/'输出'? In StageRail's 实时输出 block, each row shows '思考'/'输出' based on kind. But our stream buffer keys by channel only — so reasoning and text chunks accumulate into the same buffer per channel, and the kind of the *last* chunk decides the label. Hmm: `streams: Record<string, StreamBuffer>` where StreamBuffer has `kind`. So reasoning chunks then text chunks overwrite kind → the label flips to 输出 and the buffer mixes thinking + answer. That's a design gap for reasoner: need to split by channel+kind (e.g., key = channel + ':' + kind).

That's a real code change: state.ts (key by kind), liveChannels, streamText(channel, kind), ClaimBoard liveText usage (answer/verdict should show 输出; thinking shown in StageRail only), tests.

Plan for P1-2:
- live.ts: ensure `reasoning_content` handled (already).
- Add model selection: LLM_MODEL default stays deepseek-chat; document that deepseek-reasoner also works (CROSSEXAM_MODEL). Add `reasoning` config? Simpler: user sets LLM_MODEL=deepseek-reasoner.
- Capability tweak: when model includes 'reasoner', don't send response_format json_object (since unsupported) — send json:false but still instruct JSON in prompt (already instructed) and rely on fence stripping. Hmm, but the fingerprint includes json flag → new cassettes. And replay cassettes are recorded with json:true for chat. If someone switches model in replay mode... the digest doesn't include model, so the *same* cassette would be used regardless of model. That's by design (model-agnostic). But if reasoner sends no response_format, the request shape differs (json:false vs true) → digest changes → demo cassettes (recorded with json:true) won't match for a reasoner run. That's acceptable: in replay mode the request must match the recorded shape. But then a reasoner user in replay mode gets cassette-missing... Our demo cassettes are the offline path; if the default model becomes reasoner, replay breaks unless re-recorded with json:false. Careful.

Simplest robust approach: **keep json:true for all stages** (works with deepseek-chat), and add a separate opt-in: if model name contains 'reasoner', omit response_format (json:false). Demo/replay path stays with deepseek-chat (json:true) → cassettes unchanged. Document in README: "想看模型思考过程就用 LLM_MODEL=deepseek-reasoner（联网模式）".

P1-1 (verdict口径): Look at verdict prompt: it asks for kind/confidence/rationale/evidenceQuotes. The observed problem: model判 unanswerable for claims where nobody answered (correct per our convergence rule!) — wait. Our code-level rule: ALL_UNANSWERED → force unanswerable. In live runs, the 求职者 Agent answered "简历里没写" (answered:false) for most probes because the resume lacked details. So verdicts being unanswerable is *correct per the rules*: the claim couldn't be defended. Hmm, but is the *product口径* right? Judgment kinds: 站得住/表述过度/证据缺失/与原文矛盾/无法回答.

The observed oddity: C8 (timeline) judged unanswerable although the model wrote "时间线主张本身有原文直接支撑，站得住". Which rule fired? Probably the candidate didn't answer the timeline probes → ALL_UNANSWERED forced unanswerable. Hmm, so the candidate agent failed to answer questions about dates present in the resume → that's an **answer-agent** weakness (it should answer from the resume: dates are there!). Actually a timeline claim "2023.06-2025.03 某电商" CAN be answered from the resume (the dates are in the resume). If the candidate said "答不上来", that's the answer agent being too conservative.

So P1-1 should be reframed: the issue is not only verdict prompt but the **answer agent** (must answer when the resume contains the fact) and the **verdict agent** (should judge "证据缺失" when the resume states it but no detail, vs "无法回答" when nothing could be said). The forced-convergence rule "all unanswered → unanswerable" may be too blunt: if the claim is directly grounded in the resume text (anchor exists, exact=true) and the probes weren't answered, maybe "unsupported"(证据缺失) is more honest... Hmm. Let me think about product semantics:

- 站得住 solid: the resume + the candidate's answers hold up.
- 表述过度 overstated: the resume overstates relative to evidence.
- 证据缺失 unsupported: no evidence in the resume for the claim.
- 与原文矛盾 contradicts: the answer contradicts the resume.
- 无法回答 unanswerable: the candidate could not answer the probes.

If the resume contains the dates for a timeline claim, then judging "unanswerable" is confusing. But if the candidate answers "journey dates are in the resume" it'd be answered → then verdict could be solid/unsupported.

So the real fix is in the **answer agent prompt**: instruct it to answer whenever the resume contains the fact, even if only partially, and put the exact quote; only set answered:false when the resume truly has nothing. And the answer-agent's answers currently cite quotes; the resume HAS the dates → so it should be able to answer.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have the prompts. Let me analyze the P1-1 issue more precisely, ideally with real data. Let me look at the earlier live run report (14965 chars) if it's still on disk (.tmp/live5 or similar). Let me check .tmp for artifacts.

Also let me consider: what exactly to fix in the "判定口径"?

Key hypothesis about the live-run result (9-10 claims, nearly all unanswerable):
1. The **answer agent** produces answered:false too often because the probes ask for details not in the resume (e.g. "2.4s 怎么测的") — the resume doesn't say, so answered:false is *correct*.
2. Then verdict: ALL_UNANSWERED → forced unanswerable. But the verdict system prompt's口径 says:
   - unsupported = 简历只有结论、没有过程或口径，回答也补不上
   - unanswerable = 连续追问后仍无法给出任何有效信息
   These two overlap! For "只有结论没有口径" claims (the 2.4s case), the résumé*cáse* matches unsupported, and also "追问后无法给出有效信息" matches unanswerable. The model's own preference then matters, and our convergence forces unanswerable.
   
   Product-wise the more informative verdict for "简历只写了结果、说不出过程" is **unsupported（证据缺失）**, because the actionable output is "改简历：补上口径". "无法回答" implies the candidate couldn't answer — which is also true but less actionable. And the suggests stage treats both the same (both suggestible).
   
   So P1-1 = 让「简历写了结论但答不出过程」落到 unsupported，而「连问题本身都无法回应/完全说不出任何信息」才落 unanswerable。Two changes:
   a) prompts.ts VERDICT_SYSTEM: sharpen the boundary between unsupported and unanswerable (unsupported = 简历有这句话但缺口径/过程/可核验信息；unanswerable = 连主张本身都无法确认，或连续答非所问/自相矛盾到无法判定).
   b) verdict.ts convergence: currently "all probes unanswered (answered:false) → force unanswerable". Change to: all unanswered → if the claim is grounded in the resume text (always true, since anchors are required) → 降级 unsupported? Hmm, careful: unanswerable is a legit category for claims like "学习能力强" (self-evaluation) where the candidate真答不上来，但是... also "证据缺失" fits ("简历只有结论").
   
   Hmm, is unanswerable then ever used? It exists in the domain. Perhaps define: unanswerable = 所有追问都没答上来 **且** 判定所需的证据在简历里也找不到（即既没有回答、简历也没有依据）。unsupported = 简历有依据/结论但缺口径，回答也补不上细节。
   
   Hmm, both look similar. Let me think about what a *user* wants to see:
   - "站得住" ✓
   - "表述过度" → 降级用词
   - "证据缺失" → 简历没写清楚（补细节/删掉）
   - "与原文矛盾" → 回答和简历打架
   - "无法回答" → 这人真不会（危险，需要补课/删掉这条）
   
   Distinction: 证据缺失 = 简历表述问题（可改写解决）；无法回答 = 候选人知识问题（改写也救不了，需要真会）。For "学习能力强、抗压能力强" — that's unverifiable but the candidate *can* answer with an example (if real) → answer agent said "简历里没写例子" → answered:false → 那条是不是"无法回答"？按上面口径应该是 unsupported（表述问题）还是 unanswerable？I'd say unsupported（简历里只有形容词，没有佐证）。
   
   So essentially: **all-unanswered → unsupported** becomes the norm, and unanswerable becomes rare (e.g., 候选人明确说"我就是不会"). That makes unanswerable nearly unreachable → its convergence rule becomes dead code and the demo (which deliberately produces 2 unanswerable) would change.
   
   Alternative, less invasive: keep unanswerable for all-unanswered, but **fix the answer agent** to answer when the resume actually contains the material. In the timeline case the dates ARE in the resume — the answer agent answered "简历里只写了结果" for the metric claims (correct) but for timeline claims? From summary: 判定 C2 unanswerable / C8 (timeline) unanswerable with model rationale "时间线主张本身有原文直接支撑，站得住" yet judged unanswerable → because probes unanswered? If the answer agent had answered "2023.06-2025.03 在某某公司，简历上就是这么写的" then it'd be answered, and verdict could be solid.
   
   So the deeper bug: **probe questions ask for facts not in the resume and the answer agent gives up entirely (answered:false) rather than answering what it CAN** (e.g., "简历上写的是 2023.06–2025.03；具体项目内容简历没写"). Our current ANSWER_SYSTEM rule 2 encourages binary giving-up. A better口径: **尽量回答，能答多少答多少**：如果简历里有部分依据，就用那部分回答并在 text 里说明"简历里没写的部分我说不了"; only answered:false when the resume truly contains nothing relevant.
   
   That's a great, low-risk product improvement: it makes many verdicts "站得住/证据缺失" instead of blanket "无法回答", and it's more realistic (a real candidate says "简历上是这么写的，细节我可以说说项目背景"). Combined with也可能是 verdict口径 sharsening.

   Risk: the candidate agent might then *invent* detail. Guard: it must cite quotes, and 编造引文被丢弃; plus prompts forbid inventing. Some risk of hallucinated specifics — mitigate by adding "不能编造简历中没有的数字或事实；如果问题问的是简历没写的细节，明确说这部分简历没有写，但把简历里有的部分说清楚。"

3. Also the extract agent produced 8-10 claims including 自我评价 (「学习能力强」) — for a real resume like <REDACTED:local>'s (lots of 参赛/获奖 with no results), extraction will produce plenty.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

This is a goldmine of evidence. The core problem is crystal clear now:

**The model says in its own rationale "判定为证据缺失" (unsupported) but the code then rewrites it to unanswerable** — because of the convergence rule in verdict.ts: "all probes answered:false → force unanswerable". Look at C1: model rationale explicitly says "属于简历只有结论、没有过程或口径，回答也补不上，判定为证据缺失而非矛盾" → yet adjusted:true, adjustmentReason "候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出有效回答，按「无法回答」处理" → kind = unanswerable.

Same for C5, C6, C7, C12. So 5 of the unanswerables are **code-induced**, not model choices. The model wanted unsupported every time.

Also note: the answer agent isn't giving answered:false — wait, answeredStats said answered:18. But the droppedEvidence shows citations that failed to locate: e.g. "具体是按什么口径算的…简历里都没有写" — the answer agent's citedQuotes presumably didn't match, so answered was... hmm. Actually `answered` is a runtime bool in the draft (answered:boolean && text.trim().length>0). droppedEvidence is the Verdict's droppedEvidence (evidence quotes that didn't locate). The adjustmentReason says "候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出有效回答" — meaning ALL_UNANSWERED_REASON triggered, meaning all probes for that claim had `answered === false` in the runtime record. But answeredStats computed from view.probes[].answer.text — and the answer text is non-empty... 

Hmm wait, the runtime `answered` bool is recorded where? recordAnswer(budget, claimId, answered) uses the runtime bool, and verdict.ts checks transcript. In pipeline/run, transcriptByClaim holds probes+answers including the runtime answered bool. The stored Answer only has text+mode (+cites in v3). So a probe can have non-empty text but answered=false (the model wrote an explanation but set answered:false). Indeed the answer agent's rule 2 encourages answered:false when it can't give a real answer, yet it still writes explanatory text ("简历里没有写…我这边没法给出具体信息"). answeredStats counted those as 'answered' because it only looked at text length. So actually MANY probes were answered:false with explanatory text.

So there are two compounding issues:
1. The answer agent sets answered:false whenever it can't fully answer, even while providing a reasonable response.
2. The convergence rule then forces unanswerable for all-unanswered claims — overriding the model's own (correct) unsupported judgment.

The product semantics: 「无法回答」means the candidate couldn't answer at all — heavy. 「证据缺失」means the resume lacks evidence — actionable. Almost everything here is the latter, so nearly all claims get slammed to unanswerable, making the product's output monotonous and less useful... Actually wait: is it wrong? For C1 the model wanted unsupported; the code forced unanswerable. Fix = rethink the convergence rule.

What should the rule be? The purpose of ALL_UNANSWERED convergence was: if the candidate answered nothing, don't let the model call it "站得住" (solid) — because solid requires evidence that the candidate could back up. But it's too blunt to force unanswerable.

Better rule: if all probes for a claim were answered:false (no effective answer):
- If the model says solid → downgrade (to unsupported, since there's no corroboration beyond the resume's own words... hmm, but "站得住" for a certificate claim with no probes is legit).
- Keep the model's choice if it's unsupported/overstated/contradicts? The model rationale already explains why.

Hmm. Then what would unanswerable ever be? Design decision: unanswerable should mean "候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出任何有效信息，且简历原文也不足以支撑" — which is basically the same as unsupported here.

Let me think about the product's five verdicts as a *resume-improvement* diagnostic:
- solid 站得住: resume语句 + (回答 or 可核验事实) 撑得住 → 保留
- overstated 表述过度: 用词比证据强 → 改小
- unsupported 证据缺失: 简历只有结论，没有口径/过程/数字 → 补细节
- contradicts 与原文矛盾: 回答与简历或其他材料打架 → 统一口径
- unanswerable 无法回答: 候选人本人也说不出来，且简历也没写 → 这条要么补学要么删

Distinguishing unsupported vs unanswerable: 
- unsupported: 简历写了但没写清楚；候选人能说一点/或干脆没有追问；核心动作是"把简历写具体"。
- unanswerable: 追问了、候选人反复答不上来 → 这条能力/经历可能不实，风险最高；动作是"要么补上真实经历，要么删掉"。

With that口径, the current convergence (all unanswered → unanswerable) is defensible! C5「能够独立进行故障诊断」追问两次都没案例 → 无法回答（危险信号）is arguably right, more alarming than 证据缺失.

Hmm. So which is it? Both readings exist. The real defect then is the **model's own inconsistency**: the model *wants* unsupported and explains why, and the code overrides without the user seeing it. But the UI does show the adjustment reason ("这条判定不是模型直接给的：候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出有效回答，按「无法回答」处理") — that's honest and visible. So it's not hidden.

Hmm, so maybe the real problem is different: the answer agent gives up too readily (answered:false while it CAN say something), which triggers the all-unanswered convergence. If the answer agent answered honestly ("简历上写的是 X；具体口径简历没写"), then answered=true, convergence doesn't fire, and the model's own unsupported/solid judgment stands.

Key insight: the answer agent's `answered:false` + explanatory text is contradictory: it says "答不上来" while writing a real answer. The domain model Answer has no `answered` field precisely because it's runtime-only; the answer agent should set answered:true when it provides a substantive response (even a partial one: "简历里写的是…；细节没写"), and answered:false only when it truly can't say anything about the question at all.

Wait but there's a nuance: the product wants to detect "候选人对这条主张答不上来" — a real signal. If we make the answer agent always answer, we lose the signal. Hmm.

Let me reconsider what matters for the competition and the user's P1-1 request: "判定口径（真实模型抽 8-10 条主张，几乎全判「无法回答」...）". The user perceives it as a problem. 5/12 unanswerable + 5/12 unsupported = 10/12 negative. Is that wrong? For this resume, honestly... the resume is thin: lots of "熟悉 X" lists, 结论式表述. So negative verdicts are *correct*! The problem isn't that it's negative; it's the **monotony** (nearly all the same) and the **code override** (unanswerable injected mechanically).

I think the right fix, defensibly:
1. **Sharpen the boundary** in VERDICT_SYSTEM: make explicit that 「无法回答」只用于候选人承认自己讲不出来、且这不是简历写法问题；「证据缺失」用于简历写法含糊/缺口径（最常见）。Actually the model already does this — it consistently prefers unsupported and explains the difference. So maybe the model's口径 is fine, and the fault is purely the code rule.

2. **Fix the convergence rule**: rather than unconditionally forcing unanswerable, use the answer agent's actual signal: force unanswerable only when the candidate answered nothing AND the model didn't already pick a "worse" verdict (overstated/contradicts). Hmm, or better: keep forcing but only when the model chose solid? Let me design:

Current code:
```
if (allUnanswered && kind !== 'unanswerable') → kind = 'unanswerable', reason = ALL_UNANSWERED_REASON
```
Proposal:
```
if (allUnanswered) {
  if (kind === 'solid') → 'unsupported' (简历本身不足以支撑，且候选人没能补充证据)  // 防止"没证据也算站得住"
  else if (kind === 'overstated') → keep? hmm
  else keep model's kind
}
```
Hmm, that makes unanswerable purely model-chosen (which the model rarely does). Then the verdict "无法回答" almost disappears — is that OK? It's in the domain, used by demo cassettes (2 unanswerable), the UI has a color for it. If it becomes rare, fine — but then the demo (scripted) would differ from live behavior... the demo is synthetic anyway.

Alternatively, better semantics: **unanswerable = 追问了，候选人答不上来，但简历原文本身也没给出任何可核验的东西** — that's the same as unsupported... ugh.

Let me step back and think about what single change gives the biggest product improvement with least risk, and is honest:

**Root cause found: the answer agent sets answered:false while writing a substantive "简历里没写" response, which mechanically drags every such claim to 无法回答 via the code rule, overriding the verdict model's own "证据缺失" judgment.** 

Two-part fix:
(a) **answer.ts prompt**: make the semantics of `answered` precise — answered=true 当且仅当候选人给出了实质回应（包括"简历里写的是…，细节简历没写"这种如实说明）；只有完全无法对该问题作出任何回应（连简历里的相关内容都答不上、或问题本身无法理解/不相关）才 answered=false. Also require: answered=false 时 text 仍要写清为什么答不上来. Hmm — but careful: the 'gave-up' signal is valuable ("候选人自己也说不出来"). With (a), the signal becomes: answered=false only for truly no-response.

Hmm, wait. Does the answer agent's answered=false here matter at all? Look at the data: for C1 all 2 probes → adjustmentReason fired → so answered=false for both. Yes it matters — it's the trigger.

(b) **verdict.ts convergence**: make it less blunt — when all probes unanswered, don't force unanswerable regardless; instead:
   - if model said solid → downgrade to unsupported（简历原文之外没有佐证）— keeps the anti-hallucination intent
   - otherwise keep the model's verdict（它已经给了理由）
   Hmm, but then unanswerable is only when the model itself picks it. Does the model ever? In this run, zero. So the verdict kind would become near-dead.

Is that acceptable/correct? Let me think about the semantics I'd defend in the technical doc:
- **证据缺失 (unsupported)**: 简历里这句话缺少可核验的过程/口径/数字 → 修改建议：把口径补上。
- **无法回答 (unanswerable)**: 追问之后，候选人给不出任何有效信息，**而且这不是简历写法的问题** —— 例如问题针对的是简历里根本没提过的东西？Hmm,但所有追问都必须锚定简历原文……

Alternative framing: 无法回答 = 候选人的回答与问题无关/回避/反复兜圈子，或者候选人明确说"我不会"（能力缺失），而不是"简历没写"。That's distinguishable: 「我不会 ceph」 vs 「简历里没写 ceph 细节」。

Indeed! In the C6 case, the answer agent's dropped evidence says "简历里只写了熟悉ceph和zabbix的安装配置与排错，但具体几个节点、副本数设多少、OSD掉线怎么定位到具体哪块盘，这些细节简历里没有写，我不能凭空编" → that's "简历没写细节", not "我不会" → 证据缺失 is right.
For C5「能够独立进行故障诊断」 the candidate said "简历里没有写具体的硬件故障排查案例…这块我确实答不上来" → 一半是简历没写，一半是"我确实答不上来"。

So: the discriminator is whether the candidate's inability stems from the resume's vagueness (→ unsupported: fix the resume) or from lacking the ability/experience (→ unanswerable: this claim is dangerous). That's genuinely hard for a model to call, but the model DOES call it (it prefers unsupported).

Decision: I'll do a combination that keeps the code-level safety net but stops the mechanical override:

If the candidate couldn't back it up at all, and the model thought it stood on the resume text alone... For a certificate claim (C9 solid, no probes) there's no all-unanswered trigger. But if a claim has probes all unanswered and model says solid → the safest honest verdict is 证据缺失 (简历只有结论) — hmm, or 无法回答? I'd say 证据缺失 is more actionable and less accusatory. Hmm, but the original intent of ALL_UNANSWERED was to catch exactly this: model being lenient. Downgrading solid→unsupported still catches it. 

Rule B (new): all-unanswered + model said unanswerable/unsupported/overstated/contradicts → keep the model's verdict (it gave a witnessed rationale), but append a note to the adjustment reason? The UI shows adjustmentReason only when `adjusted`. Hmm — maybe set `adjusted=false` but keep a "note"? Verdict type has `adjusted: boolean` and `adjustmentReason`. Adding a note field means domain change + UI + tests. Simpler: if the model's kind is retained, keep adjusted=false (no adjustment happened).

Hmm, but then how does the "candidate couldn't answer" signal surface? It's visible in the probe cards (答不上来 badge per probe). Yes! The per-probe answer badge already shows 答不上来. Good — the signal is preserved at probe granularity, and the claim verdict stays the model's.

Wait, but with fix (a) (answer agent semantics), how many probes would be answered:false? Those where the candidate gives a substantive "简历里没写…" answer → answered=true. That also removes the mechanical trigger in most cases. Both changes independently reduce the false unanswerable.

Hmm, careful: change (a) makes 'gave-up' probes rare, which weakens the "无法回答" verdict further, and also weakens budget's maxConsecutiveUnanswerable auto-close (which triggers on answered:false → closing claims after 2 consecutive). In the baseline: answeredStats all 18 answered... wait no. Let me recheck: the rule "consecutiveUnanswerable >= 2 → closed" — did any claim get closed in the baseline? Let me check claims[].status / closeReason. probes=18 across 12 claims, rounds=3. If many claims were closed early, that'd show. Not critical.

Hold on. Let me reconsider (a). Is it right to call "简历里没写，我讲不出来" an *answer*? Product-wise, the verdict stage needs to know: did the candidate provide new information? The `answered` bool is used for (1) budget closing, (2) verdict convergence, (3) UI badge. If we flip "简历没写" to answered=true, then the UI badge shows 「求职者作答」 for a non-answer — misleading.

Better: **keep answered as-is** (it means "候选人给出了有效回答"), and fix the convergence rule only, plus sharpen the prompt口径. That's simpler, lower-risk, and directly addresses the override. And the UI badge stays honest.

But then, with convergence relaxed, what decides unsupported vs unanswerable? The verdict model, guided by a sharpened口径. The model already distinguishes well (see rationales: it explicitly says "属于证据缺失而非矛盾"). To make it pick unanswerable when appropriate, I need to sharpen the口径 so that "候选人自己也承认讲不出来" → 无法回答, while "简历没写细节但候选人如实说明" → hmm, these overlap again... 

Honestly, maybe the cleanest product口径:

- **证据缺失**: 简历只有结论、没有口径，但**候选人能补充说明**（回答有效）→ 只需把简历写具体。

That's a clean discriminator based on a signal the code already has: **是否所有追问都没答上来**! 
- all unanswered (and there was ≥1 probe) → unanswerable
- otherwise, if resume lacks evidence → unsupported

And that's exactly what the current code does... except the code overrides the model. So the "fix" would be to keep the code rule but make it *primary*, and align the prompt so the model agrees. Hmm! Then the observed behavior (5 unanswerable) is... by design?

Wait, was C1 all-unanswered? Yes (adjustmentReason fired). C1 = 成绩前3%并获得企业奖学金 — the candidate answered "简历里没写口径/学期/企业…我这边没法确认". Two genuine attempts, both non-answers in substance. So per the clean discriminator, 无法回答 is defensible: the candidate could not substantiate it in an interview.

Hmm, so is anything actually broken? The user's complaint was "几乎全判无法回答". Baseline: 5 unanswerable / 5 unsupported / 2 solid. Not "全" — but 10/12 negative.

Hmm, hold on. Maybe the real problem is upstream: **the answer agent is too weak** — it's told to answer strictly from the resume, and it treats "简历里没写" as a full stop. A real candidate would answer: "成绩前 3% 是按专业同年级总人数排名算的，是我们学院统一出的排名；具体学期是 2023-2024 学年。" — a real person knows these things without the resume. The agent's rule "只依据简历作答" makes it a *resume reader*, not a *candidate*. That's why every probing question ends in "简历里没写". 

THAT is the real product flaw: **求职者 Agent 被约束成"只能说简历里写过的"，所以任何追问都会撞上"简历里没写"**，于是所有主张都被判证据缺失/无法回答。而真实的面试里，候选人是会补充口头信息的。

But wait — the product's core value is 「反推简历」: the fact-account philosophy says only verified facts enter. Here it's not generation though, it's *pressure-testing*: the candidate agent simulates what the candidate will sound like. If the candidate agent can embellish, the verdicts lose meaning (it'd always satisfy the interviewer). The tension: the candidate agent *should* be able to add details that a real candidate would say, but those details are unverifiable → the verdict would be "回答补充了口径" which is exactly the actionable finding: "简历里没写，但你能说清楚 → 补进简历"! 

That's a genuinely better product: 
- If the candidate can supplement → verdict could be `solid`（回答能撑住）but the *suggestion* is "把面试里说的这段补进简历". Hmm, adds a new suggestion category.

That's a bigger redesign than P1-1 scope. Careful about scope creep and time (23 days left, user wants P1 three items + video).

Let me settle on a pragmatic P1-1 with clear, defensible improvements:

**Change 1 (prompt, verdict口径)**: sharpen unsupported vs unanswerable:
- unsupported（证据缺失）：简历里这句话缺过程/口径/数字，**候选人也没能补充**，但这不是"不会"，而是"没写下来"——最常见的判定。
- unanswerable（无法回答）：候选人明确表示**自己不具备**这项能力/经历，或追问后连"简历里写了什么"都对不上（含糊其辞、答非所问）。不要因为"简历没写"就判无法回答。
This makes the model's own preference (unsupported) authoritative and reserves unanswerable for real incapacity.

**Change 2 (verdict.ts convergence)**: replace "all unanswered → force unanswerable" with:
- all unanswered + model said `solid` → downgrade to `unsupported`（reason 改为「候选人对这条主张的追问都没能补充任何有效信息，不足以判为站得住」）
- all unanswered + model said others → keep (no override)
So the code no longer injects unanswerable mechanically; it still prevents an unsupported "solid".

**Change 3 (answer agent, small)**: allow the candidate to answer with what a real candidate knows **only about their own resume's content** and explicitly say when a detail isn't in the resume. Hmm — actually current behavior already does this. Skip?

**Idea (Change 3, the real fix)**: give the candidate agent a role: it may answer as the candidate, using the resume as its source of truth for facts, and it MAY supply 口径/细节 that a real candidate would say **but must mark them as not in the resume**? OK so let me reconsider: maybe the real fix should be on the **answer side**: the current answer agent gives answered:false too eagerly — it says "简历里没写" and stops, while writing explanatory text. The demo's C3/C4 answers are the same pattern ("简历里没有写这些业务线的具体情况…"). If instead the candidate agent is allowed to reason as a *real candidate* would — "简历里没写，但我可以说：这 12 条业务线是订单、支付、风控…" — then answered=true and richer transcripts, and verdicts become more differentiated (maybe contradicts when the interview exposes inconsistency).

But that requires the candidate agent to invent facts not in the resume, which risks hallucination and undermines the "只落本地/不编造" ethos... though here it's *simulation*, and the whole point of the pressure test is that a real candidate WILL say things. However, the interview agent's answers are then unverifiable, and the verdict stage can't check them against the resume. Yet the verdict stage does exactly that: "回答与简历原文冲突 → contradicts". If the candidate invents "12 条业务线是订单、支付…", that's not a contradiction.

Hmm, this is a deep design question and the user's P1-1 wording was about "判定口径". I should make a decision that's defensible, improves usefulness, and keeps the demo rich.

**Decision**: Reframe the five verdicts around *what the user should do*, and make the discriminator explicit and enforced in code:

- solid 站得住: 有原文事实 + 回答没崩 → 保留
- overstated 表述过度: 有事实底子但用词过强 → 降级用词
- unsupported 证据缺失: 简历只有结论没有口径/过程 → **补细节**（这是简历写法问题）
- contradicts 与原文矛盾: 回答和简历打架 → 统一口径
- unanswerable 无法回答: 追问了，候选人**连"简历里写了什么"都对不上**、或明确承认不具备 → **这条最危险，要么补真经历要么删**

And then: **all-unanswered → unanswerable is genuinely defensible** under this framing ("候选人拿不出任何有效信息"). The problem the user saw isn't that unanswerable is wrong, but that it's *over-applied* because **the answer agent treats "简历没写细节" as "答不上来"**, when a real candidate would at least restate what they did and own the detail level.

So the honest fix is on the ANSWER side, and it's small: teach the answer agent the difference between "简历里没写，但我记得/我能说" and "我确实不知道/没做过". But we can't let it invent verifiable facts... Actually wait. Think about the product again: the candidate agent is meant to *simulate the user answering*. The user is the real person; the agent doesn't know their real details. So "只依据简历作答" is a deliberate, honest constraint: the simulator can't know more than the resume. Given that constraint, "简历里没写" is the only honest answer — so all-unanswered is *inevitable* for any claim whose details aren't in the resume. And then unanswerable is inevitable. Which makes unsupported/unanswerable synonyms again → the mechanical rule just decides.

Hmm! Unless... the candidate agent is allowed to answer from the resume + **the JD** + reasonable professional knowledge? No.

OK. Let me flip to the *product* framing that resolves this cleanly:

The output's value = "哪些话会在面试里被问穿，以及为什么". The five kinds are diagnostic labels. The real defect the user noticed: **12 条主张里 10 条都是负面的、且判定几乎只有两种**. Is that a defect, or the truth about this resume? Looking at the resume: 8 条个人总结全是"熟悉 X"堆砌、项目只有一句"负责全部流程"、获奖没有名次细节 → honestly yes, 10/12 negative is *correct and valuable*. A judge would find that convincing: the tool found a weak resume.

So the actual improvement for P1-1 should be **differentiation and actionability**, not "make it less negative":
1. The model's own judgment should win where it disagrees with the mechanical rule (that's a real code-level defect: model says 证据缺失 with a solid rationale, code overrides to 无法回答 — and the *rationale text shown to the user stays the model's*, so the UI shows "属于证据缺失" while displaying the badge 「无法回答」! **That's an actual inconsistency the user sees.**). Let me verify: VerdictBlock shows VerdictBadge(kind, adjusted) + rationale + if adjusted: 「这条判定不是模型直接给的：{adjustmentReason}」. The rationale shown for C1 says "...判定为证据缺失而非矛盾" while the badge says 无法回答. The explanation line clarifies, but it's still a bad look.
2. Make unanswerable *model-chosen* only, and give the model a clear口径 for it (candidate admits incapacity).
3. To keep the demo rich, **change the demo script** so that C3/C4 verdicts are genuinely unanswerable: the candidate answers "我不会/我确实没做过" for one, and the demo keeps all five kinds representable. C3 = RBAC 独立完成: candidate says "业务线量级我确实不记得了" hmm. Let me make C4 (学习能力强/抗压能力强) a true unanswerable: answer answered:false, text "抗压这个我确实举不出例子，这块我说不上来" + verdict kind 'unanswerable' explicitly. Then the demo shows all five kinds: overstated (C1), solid (C2), unsupported (C3), unanswerable (C4), unsupported→? C5 获奖 (solid draft with no evidence → downgraded unsupported). Fine: ['overstated','solid','unsupported','unanswerable','unsupported'] — still 5 kinds? No: solid, overstated, unsupported, unanswerable = 4 kinds (unsupported twice). The UI screenshot 06-verdicts highlighted C5「证据缺失 · 代码收敛」. That stays. Good enough. Actually the earlier demo had unanswerable×2 and no... let me recount current: overstated(C1), solid(C2), unanswerable(C3), unanswerable(C4), unsupported(C5) → 4 distinct. New: overstated, solid, unsupported(C3), unanswerable(C4), unsupported(C5) → 4 distinct. Same richness!

So a live run giving mostly 证据缺失 + a few 站得住 is correct and consistent.

117| export function listCassettes(dir: string): CassetteSummary[] {
118|   if (!existsSync(dir)) return [];
119|   const out: CassetteSummary[] = [];
120|   for (const name of readdirSync(dir).sort()) {
121|     if (!name.endsWith('.json')) continue;

Test code:

```ts
  it('重跑生成器会清掉旧指纹的合成卡带，但不动真实录制的卡带', async () => {
    const dir = newTempDir('demo-prune');
    const first = await seedDemoCassettes({ dir });
    expect(first.pruned).toEqual([]);

    // 造两盘这一轮不再需要的卡带：一盘是旧的合成卡带（孤儿），一盘是真人用
    // CROSSEXAM_MODE=record 录下来的真实卡带——后者一根汗毛都不许动。
    const staleDigest = 'a'.repeat(24);
    const realDigest = 'b'.repeat(24);
    writeCassette(dir, fakeEntry(staleDigest, 'scripted-demo'));
    writeCassette(dir, fakeEntry(realDigest, 'deepseek-chat'));

    const second = await seedDemoCassettes({ dir });

    expect(second.pruned).toEqual([staleDigest]);
    const left = listCassettes(dir).map((item) => item.digest);
    expect(left).not.toContain(staleDigest);
    expect(left).toContain(realDigest);
    expect(left).toHaveLength(first.entries.length + 1);
  });
```

And helper fakeEntry at module level:

```ts
function fakeEntry(digest: string, model: string): CassetteEntry {

    recordedAt: '2026-10-26T00:00:00.000Z',
    request: { system: 'x', messages: [], temperature: null, maxTokens: null, json: true },
    response: { text: '{}', usage: EMPTY_USAGE },
  };
}
```

Before: 7 unanswerable (including ones the model's text said were 证据缺失) → misleading badge. Now: all unsupported with reasons that match. The user-visible difference: 建议 actionable. I think it's an improvement, but "全部都是同一档" is a new criticism (判定区分度不足).

Should I refine further? The remaining question is whether unanswerable should be rare. Semantically: unanswerable = 候选人当场答不上/拿不出 → in live runs the answer agent almost always answers with "简历里没写" (answered:true with text) → so hardly ever unanswerable. Actually the answer agent is playing the candidate based only on the resume; it can never "not know" things beyond the resume, so unanswerable is legitimately rare. That's coherent: with an honest candidate agent that only knows the resume, "本人拿不出" is rare. Good — and it explains why the old rule was wrong twice over.

I'll note this in findings as the insight: 求职者 Agent 只依据简历作答，所以「本人拿不出」本就罕见；把大量主张判成无法回答等于把简历的问题算在候选人头上。

02-intent-control.md 2.6.1 table: rewrite both rows and add the "责任在谁" 判据 paragraph. Let me draft:

```
### 2.6.1 判定阶段的两条收敛规则

模型判完之后，`enforceVerdict` 会用两条确定性规则复核（`verdict.ts`）：

| 规则 | 条件 | 处理 | 理由文案 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 全没答上来还判站得住 | 有追问，所有追问的回答都无效，且模型判「站得住」 | 降级为「证据缺失」 | 候选人对这条主张的追问没能落出口径，原文这一句不足以判为「站得住」，按「证据缺失」处理 |
| 要证据却拿不出 | 判为「站得住」或「与原文矛盾」，但证据引文一条都定位不到 | 降级为「证据缺失」 | 模型判为「站得住」但模型拿不出可定位到简历原文的证据，按「证据缺失」处理 |

第二条尤其重要：**「站得住」是一个需要证据的结论**。模型想判 solid，就必须拿出能在简历里定位到的引文；拿不出，这个判定就不成立。这与 L2 的锚定是同一个思想，只是从「提问」延伸到了「结论」。

第一条只做一件事：**防止过于乐观**，而不是替模型决定责任归属。代码手里只有一个布尔（这一场有没有答上来过），
而模型看得见每一轮问答——它比这个布尔更清楚该算「简历写得太笼统」（证据缺失，改简历就行）还是
「候选人自己拿不出东西」（无法回答，风险最高）。把「全体未答」一律改判「无法回答」的写法犯过一个真实错误：
模型的理由里明明白白写着「只有结论、没有口径，属证据缺失」，界面上却挂着「无法回答」的徽章——
理由是模型写的，结论是代码改的，两者互相矛盾（这一条由真实联网运行发现，见第五章 5.6）。

为了让这两档可判别，VERDICT_SYSTEM 把判据写成了「责任在谁」：**证据缺失 = 简历的错**（这句话写得太笼统，
改法是把口径写进去）；**无法回答 = 人的错**（本人承认不记得、举不出例子、没做过，面试现场会直接翻车）。
拿不准时判「证据缺失」——它是一个可执行的结论，指向改简历；而误判「无法回答」会让人以为这段经历该删掉。
```

### 2.6.1 判定阶段的两条收敛规则

模型判完之后，`enforceVerdict` 会用两条确定性规则复核（`verdict.ts`）：

| 规则 | 条件 | 处理 | 理由文案 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 全部答不上来 | 有追问，且所有追问的回答都无效 | 强制改判「无法回答」 | 候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出有效回答，按「无法回答」处理 |
| 要证据却拿不出 | 判为「站得住」或「与原文矛盾」，但证据引文一条都定位不到 | 降级为「证据缺失」 | 模型判为「站得住」但模型拿不出可定位到简历原文的证据，按「证据缺失」处理 |

第二条尤其重要：**「站得住」是一个需要证据的结论**。模型想判 solid，就必须拿出能在简历里定位到的引文；
拿不出，这个判定就不成立。这与 L2 的锚定是同一个思想，只是从「提问」延伸到了「结论」。

Draft new inserted paragraph:

```
第一条只做一件事：**防止过于乐观**，而不是替模型决定责任归属。代码手里只有一个布尔（这一场有没有答上来过），
而模型看得见每一轮问答——它比这个布尔更清楚该算「简历写得太笼统」（证据缺失，改简历就行），
还是「候选人自己拿不出东西」（无法回答，风险最高）。把「全体未答」一律改判「无法回答」的版本犯过一个真实错误：
模型的理由里明明白白写着「只有结论、没有口径，属证据缺失」，界面上却挂着「无法回答」的徽章——
理由是模型写的，结论是代码改的，两者当场矛盾（真实联网运行时才发现，见 5.6 第 5 条）。

为了让这两档可判别，`VERDICT_SYSTEM` 把判据写成了**责任在谁**：证据缺失 = 简历的错（这句话写得太笼统，
改法是把口径写进去）；无法回答 = 人的错（本人承认不记得、举不出例子、没做过，面试现场会直接翻车）。
拿不准时判「证据缺失」——它是一个可执行的结论，指向改简历；误判「无法回答」会让人以为这段经历该删掉。
```

118| 2. **prepare().all() 的返回类型很宽**（unknown[]），TS 会在每一处用法上报错。

124| ## F9. 思考过程与缓存字段有两套命名，兼容层必须都认（2026-10-26）
125| 
126| 同一份 OpenAI 兼容协议，不同厂商的字段名不一致，实测：
127| 
128| - 思考过程：DeepSeek 用 delta.reasoning_content，OpenAI 用 delta.reasoning；
129| - 缓存命中：DeepSeek 用 prompt_cache_hit_tokens，OpenAI 用 prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens；
130| - 推理 token：completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens。
131| 
132| 任何只认一套的适配层，换一个模型就会静默丢掉用量或思考过程（不报错，只是数字少了）。
133| 因此 readUsage 与 parseSseData 都把两套名字一起认。
134| 这条也解释了为什么卡带指纹刻意不含模型名：换模型不该让整盘卡带失效。

142| package.json 声明了 bin，但仓库里没有那个文件，克隆下来的人一跑就报找不到。
143|

```text
=== findings 425-454
425| 修法：面板是**集合语义**，顺序不该有意义。在 `packages/web/src/contract.ts` 定义
426| `PANEL_ORDER`（tech / hr / hiring_manager / stress / domain_expert），
427| 增删一律过一遍 `nextPanel()` 做归一化，点多少次都回到同一种排列。
428| 测试锁住的是那条真实翻车路径：先删 HR 再加回来，必须回到基础面板。

Findings F31/F32 content:

## F31. 代码级收敛只该防「过于乐观」，不该替模型决定责任归属（2026-10-26）

真实的联网实测（用户提供的运维岗简历，12 条主张 / 18 次追问，全部答得上）里，5 条被代码改判成「无法回答」，
而它们的模型理由全都白纸黑字写着「属于简历只有结论、没有过程或口径……判定为证据缺失而非矛盾」。
界面上于是出现最坏的一种组合：**理由说证据缺失，徽章写无法回答**。

根因是 `enforceVerdict` 里那条「有追问且全部回答无效 → 一律改判无法回答」。它手里只有一个布尔，
却要替模型回答一个语义问题：这条主张的问题出在简历写法（证据缺失，改简历即可），
还是出在候选人身上（无法回答，风险最高）。

处置：这条规则收窄到「模型判站得住、但整场没答上来」时才降级为证据缺失——即只做**防过于乐观**。
责任归属交回模型（它看得到每一轮问答），并把两档判据写进 VERDICT_SYSTEM：证据缺失 = 简历的错，
无法回答 = 人的错，拿不准判证据缺失。

顺带得到一个结构性认识：求职者 Agent 只依据简历作答，它天然说不出「我不会」，
所以「无法回答」本就该是少数——修正前那种大面积「无法回答」等于把简历的问题算在了候选人头上。

**教训**：任何「用代码兜住模型」的规则，都要问它替换掉的是模型的哪一个判断；
替换语义判断（责任归属）与替换形式判断（引文能不能定位到）是两种风险级别完全不同的干预。

## F32. 提示词一改，旧指纹的卡带会静默退休成孤儿（2026-10-26）

改 VERDICT_SYSTEM 与演示脚本的两条作答文案之后，`npm run seed:demo` 写出 29 盘新卡带，
可目录里多出 7 个谁也读不到的旧文件：verdict 的 5 盘与 suggest 的 2 盘换了指纹
（suggest 的提示词里含问答记录，作答文案一改，它的指纹也跟着变）。
卡带按指纹寻址，读不到不是错误，于是**没有任何东西会提示这批文件已经作废**；
它们只是让「卡带共 N 盘」变成假话，并让后来的人以为某盘卡带被用上了。

处置：生成器收尾时清理「model 等于 scripted-demo 且 digest 不在本轮清单里」的卡带
（`pruned` 进报告，命令行打印条数）。只删合成卡带：用户用 CROSSEXAM_MODE=record 真录下来的
卡带模型名不是它，一根汗毛都不许动。补了一条测试：手工塞一盘旧合成卡带与一盘「真实录制」卡带，
重跑生成器后旧合成卡带消失、真实卡带还在。

**教训**：任何「按内容寻址」的缓存都会在内容改动后留下孤儿，而孤儿是静默的。
寻址机制的作者有义务提供清理，且清理范围必须由可判别的标记（这里是 model 字段）界定。

progress Session 14:

## Session 14 · P1-1 判定口径：把责任归属交回模型（2026-10-26）

用户的真实简历（12 条主张、18 次追问）跑出来的判定几乎一边倒：修正前 5 条「无法回答」，
而模型的理由里写的是「证据缺失」。核到根因是代码级收敛把「全体未答」一律改判，
吃掉了模型已经做对的判断（F31）。本轮的处置与验证：

### 改了什么

- verdict.ts：降级规则收窄为「模型判站得住但整场没答上来 → 证据缺失」，理由文案同步改写；
- prompts.ts 的 VERDICT_SYSTEM：把两档判据写成可判别的「责任在谁」——证据缺失 = 简历的错、
  无法回答 = 人的错，拿不准判证据缺失；
- scripted-demo.ts：演示里保留一个真·无法回答（自我评价那条，候选人明确承认举不出事例），
  其余走证据缺失，四种判定在离线演示里仍然齐全；
- demo-seed.ts：生成器清理旧指纹的孤儿卡带（F32），只删 model=scripted-demo 的那一批。

### 验证

- 真实简历 live A/B（同一份简历、同一个 JD、同机同日）：修正前 5 unanswerable / 5 unsupported / 2 solid，
  修正后 11 unsupported / 1 unanswerable，建议由 9 条变 12 条；
- 示例简历 live 重跑：10 条主张 / 18 次追问 / 20.0 s，判定 10 条证据缺失；
- 离线卡带重录：29 盘、两遍签名一致 df5664d4894c、判定 站得住 1 / 表述过度 1 / 证据缺失 2 / 无法回答 1；
- npm run verify exit 0：31 个测试文件 / 313 条断言 + 冒烟 9 项 + build。

### 代价（如实记录）

判定区分度下降：真实模型下这一场 10 条全是「证据缺失」。结构性的原因是求职者 Agent
只依据简历作答，说不出「我不会」。要恢复区分度得先给它一个「承认不会」的表达方式（见 6.1 第 2 条）。

  command: "npm run docs:pdf > .tmp/docs-pdf.log 2>&1; echo PDF_EXIT=$?; npm run verify > .tmp/verify2.log 2>&1; echo VERIFY_EXIT=$?; echo '--- pdf'; tail -6 .tmp/docs-pdf.log; echo '--- verify'; tail -8 .tmp/verify2.log",
  description: "Rebuild tech doc PDF and full gate",

});
if (r.kind === "foreground") {
  console.log("exit " + r.exitCode);
  console.log(r.stdout.text.slice(-2600));
  if (r.stderr.text) console.log("ERR " + r.stderr.text.slice(-500));
} else {
  console.log(JSON.stringify(r));
}

```
